# Implementation progress

## Board: switching to a group that needs you opens what waits there (branch `kanban/board-switching-to-a-group-with-somethin-f1d010`) — 2026-11-18

- Feature (user-requested): a group tab press or a switcher row opens that group's first "N need you" target (dispatcher chat, else the first need-you card with the step bar); never for the group already shown, nothing waiting, Back/Forward, links or New task from a tab menu. Spec: BOARD-GROUPS-SPEC §3, UI-REFRESH-SPEC §7ae.
- Client: pure `groupSwitchOpen` in `client/step-through.ts`; board `switchGroup` used by the tabs and the switcher rows; board history entries carry their group (`client/nav-history.ts`), so Back returns to the previous group's view. What's new 1.9.0 item (9 catalogs); no tour or tip change.

## Board: O opens the open card's active session, R its newest pull request (branch `kanban/board-shortcuts-o-opens-the-open-card-s-d549c5`) — 2026-11-18

- Feature (user-requested): two desktop board shortcuts act on the card open in the sidebar. `O` opens its active session chat (the double click's rule and entry; toast "No session yet." when there is none). `R` opens its newest pull request in the browser (toast "No pull request yet." when there is none). With no card open, or on compact layouts, neither key is consumed. Ruling (Noah, 2026-11-18): `R`, not `G`, because `G` stays Guardrails. Contract: SHORTCUTS-SPEC §2.1.
- Client: `client/shortcuts.ts` (actions `open-session` / `open-pr`, help rows, pure `cardShortcutApplies`); `newestPrUrl` in `client/quick-links.ts` (first PR chip of `quickLinks`, no second PR rule); `openQuickLink` extracted in `client/quick-links-row.tsx` and shared by the PR chip and `R`; `SessionEntry.toastNone` for `O`, resolved by the drawer's existing active-session effect; board `prRequest` prop consumed once by the drawer after the card detail loads.
- Tests: `client/shortcuts.test.ts` and `client/quick-links.test.ts` committed failing first, then passing. i18n: four strings in all 9 catalogs.
- Onboarding: no new tip or tour copy; the `?` help popover lists both keys. What's new: none (not a release).

## Board: double-click a card to jump into its active session (branch `kanban/board-double-click-a-card-to-jump-into-i-b93db9`) — 2026-11-18

- Feature (user-requested): on the desktop board a mouse double click on a card opens its card home (first click, unchanged and undelayed) and then its active session chat in the drawer. Active session = first of the Quick links order (waiting, running, error, done; newest first). No live session: stays on the card home, no toast. Touch, pen, Enter and phones unchanged. Contract: UI-UX-SPEC §3, DRAWER-SPEC Quick links.
- Client: pure `client/double-click.ts` (`isMouseDoubleClick`: `detail === 2` and mouse); `orderQuickSessions` / `activeQuickSession` in `client/quick-links.ts` (the chip sort extracted, not duplicated); `TaskCard`/`LiveTaskCard` `onOpenSession` and a last-pointer ref; board `openCardSession` sets a `SessionEntry` with `active: true` (new optional field); the drawer consumes it once this card's sessions list is known (data or error), picking `activeQuickSession(quickSessions)`. Tip `card-double-click` (9 catalogs).
- Tests: `client/quick-links.test.ts` and `client/double-click.test.ts` committed failing first, then passing; `client/onboarding-logic.test.ts` extended.
- Checks: `npm run typecheck` exit 0; `npm test` on Node 20 (no `node:sqlite`): 8 failures, all in `server/sqlite-*`, `server/store-group-*` and `scripts/single-pane-live` (Node 20 lacks `node:sqlite` / `registerHooks`), everything else passed including the i18n catalog checks.
- Real UI verification: pending (card session).
- Onboarding: one-time tip, no tour change and no What's new entry (no release). Telemetry: no heartbeat field.

- Feature (user-requested): on the web, two quick taps of Ctrl anywhere in Paseo open the Flowly board. Server only registers the setting.

The ship message names the permission prompt and asks a conflict needing a decision through the question tool.

## Auto review at the board-group level (branch `kanban/auto-review-setting-at-the-board-group-l-796638`) — 2026-11-18

- Rulings (Noah, 2026-11-18): Auto review and its rework limit are a group default (`group.defaults.autoReview`) that projects inherit; `project.autoReview` is an optional override (`null` removes it). Effective value: override, else group, else Off with 2 rounds. New projects store none. A one-time start collapse gives each group its members' most common value (ties: Off/2 when tied, else the first member), keeps differing members as overrides, and sets `board.autoReviewGroups` so it never runs again. Contract: BOARD-GROUPS-SPEC §0, §1, §2, §5, §7 step 9, §8; AUTO-REVIEW-SPEC §6, §8.1, §9.3, D19; GUARDRAILS-SPEC D1, §4.5, §8.
- Code: `shared/auto-review.ts` (`effectiveAutoReview`, `projectAutoReview`, `collapseGroupAutoReview`; `autoReviewOf`/`autoReviewEnabled`/`autoReviewRuns` removed), `shared/model.ts`, `server/store.ts` (collapse in the start write, marker, `onAutoReviewOn` hook after commits that turn a live project's effective value on), `server/service.ts`, `server/project-create.ts` and `index.server.ts` (detection only when effectively on; telemetry `f_auto_review` effective), readers in `server/agent-service.ts`, `server/auto-review.ts`, `server/guardrails.ts` (`taskContext` takes the effective value), `server/workflow-insights.ts`; client `review-settings.tsx` (*Override group Auto review*), `forms.tsx`, `group-settings.tsx` (Defaults switch and rounds, move toast), `group-settings-logic.ts`, Rerun gated by the effective value (`drawer.tsx`, `task-workpad.tsx`, `task-review.tsx`). 7 new strings and one changed tour string in all 9 catalogs; What's new 1.9.0 item; README.
- Legacy rules kept: a stored project value that still carries pre-guardrails `rules`/`checks`/`goal`/`guidance` stays as an override only while the guardrails migration is pending (`guardrails.json` missing at the start write; `migrateGroups(load, { reviewRulesPending })`), because that migration reads it after the board start write. Once guardrails exist, only enabled and rounds are compared.
- Review fix round (2026-11-18, rulings Noah): the collapse counts live members only (archived only when a group has none); a rerun on a collapsed board changes nothing (members without a value in a group that has one are skipped); un-archiving into an On group fires detection (archived projects count as off in the turn-on hook); a folder copy now carries `autoReviewInherits: true`, a copy without it restores a project with no value as an Off override, and a restore detects commands when the effective value is on (`detectWhenOn` in `server/project-create.ts`, shared with create and link). Tests failing first in daf05f8 (10 red), fixed in 44c692b.
- Tests: failing first (d17e36c): resolution, collapse cases, store/RPC set and clear, marker and no re-collapse, create stores none and detects only in an On group, the turn-on hook, a delivery reviewed by its group's value. `npm run typecheck` and `npm test` pass (227 files, 2745 tests, 7 skipped).
- Not run: UI captures before/after and a live plugin install (the brief forbade install/reload).
- Onboarding: tour legend changed from "turn it off per project" to "set it per group, and a project can override it"; no new tip (the change is visible where Auto review is already set, and What's new announces it). Telemetry: no new field; `f_auto_review` uses the effective value.

## Step-through bar always has an X (branch `kanban/unread-step-through-1-of-1-give-a-way-ou-4d0ebf`) — 2026-11-17

- Defect: with one card in a walk ("Unread · 1 of 1", typically after opening the only unread card) the bar showed two disabled arrows and no way out; Done appears only once the card leaves the queue, and the visited card stays in the unread walk (`walkQueue`), so it never did.
- Fix: `stepBarState` returns `arrows` (false with no previous and no next) and `close` (always); `StepThroughBar` hides the arrows when false and always renders an X ("Stop stepping through") that calls `endStep` (`setStep(null)`: the card stays open, nothing is marked read or snoozed). The tour's need-you demo bar gets the same X. Spec: DISPATCHER-SPEC §9.4 ruling 2026-11-17.
- Esc unchanged: it already ends the walk by closing the card sidebar (shortcuts help list says so), so no key was added.
- Tests: `client/step-through.test.ts` (failing first: the new close/arrows test over 1 of 1, 1 of N, i of N, N of N, Answered, caught up, for both kinds).
- No tip, tour page, What's new entry or telemetry field (a fix to an existing control; the tour demo's bar mirrors it).

## One live dispatcher per group (branch `kanban/one-live-dispatcher-per-group-archive-st-cc7cd8`) — 2026-11-17

- Found: d1c2d8fb (Zenlytic group) was in that group's `previousAgentIds`, but Paseo still had it live (idle) for four days next to the current dispatcher. The Tooling dispatcher replaced at the same moment (2026-11-14, after charter versioning) was archived normally. Logs from then are gone. The likely path is a single-agent lookup that returned nothing for the live background session, which `isDeadDispatcher` reads as dead, so no archive. The 16 other "closed" ids the card listed were already archived (Paseo reports an archived session as `closed`).
- `paseo archive <id>` (CLI 0.13.2) answers "Agent not found" for archived agents, as expected, and also for a live background-workspace agent: its id lookup skips background agents. That is a Paseo CLI bug and Flowly cannot fix it. The MCP `archive_agent` (and the SDK archive Flowly uses) works.
- One-time cleanup: d1c2d8fb archived with `archive_agent` after checking it had no pending permissions and that all 11 cards it created were Done. One live dispatcher per group remains (0c05baf3 Tooling, 09bac1fc Zenlytic).
- Contract: DISPATCHER-SPEC §1 (`pendingArchive`), §3 (the sweep after a start, retries, the amended archive paths), §10; AGENTS.md dispatcher invariant amended.
- Server: `sweepGroup` runs after every successful create in `runStart` (Start and Replace). It uses `liveAgents` from `server/card-chats.ts` and archives only earlier recorded ids that carry this group's dispatcher labels and are no group's current session. Failures are logged and go to `pendingArchive`. `retryPendingArchives` runs from `status()`, on the start queue, throttled by `PENDING_ARCHIVE_RETRY_MS`.
- Tests: 5 new tests in `server/dispatcher.test.ts`, written failing first (5 failed, 106 passed), then passing (111 passed).
- Other stored ids: PaseoTray (`tray/Sources/DispatcherStore.swift`) finds dispatchers live by label, keeps the newest live one per group, and keys Quick Ask recents by group id, so it stores no dispatcher id and needs no change. No bot or plugin config and no Claude memory or rule stores a dispatcher id; only history (logs, summaries, notes) mentions old ids.
- No tip, tour, What's new entry or telemetry field: this is internal behavior.
- Review round 1 (opus-reviewer, nothing blocking). Fixed as a new commit: a sweep error (including a failed record write) no longer fails the start; on a listing failure, the ids already pending are kept first; status retries back off from 1 minute up to 1 hour; each id's archive failure is logged once per distinct error; added tests for restore, withoutGroup and the current-session filter. Accepted as a known limit: a build older than this one treats a record with `pendingArchive` as invalid. Rollbacks aren't a supported path, and the field is only written after a failed archive.

## Where a follow-up card branches from (branch `kanban/default-setting-for-where-follow-up-card-0887eb`) — 2026-11-16

- Contract: START-CARD-SPEC §11 (rulings Noah 2026-11-16). `Project.followUpBase` (`parent` default, `base`, `ask`) with *Follow-ups branch from* in project settings (not for linked roots); `Task.branchedFrom`.
- Server: `server/follow-up-base.ts` (`openParents`, `chooseBase`, `rebaseLine`), git helpers `worktreeBranch` / `branchAhead` / `refResolves` in `server/base-ref.ts`. `flowly_start_card` keeps the decision with the worktree request (`base` in the kept request; Paseo gets only title, key and source), stores the project base as `Task.baseRef`, `branchedFrom` and a system `blocks` link in the move's write, and adds the rebase line to the first message. `flowly_create_card` (worktree) decides with the calling card as parent and passes the choice to `BoardService.createTask`. Both results carry `base`; replays report it again. `changeBase` takes the parent commit (§11.4). Dispatcher charter v4; tool descriptions updated.
- Decided in implementation: `baseRef: "parent"` and an explicit branch naming a parent's branch report `from: "explicit"` with `parentTaskId`; an explicit branch that does not resolve is `BASE_INVALID`; `baseRef` ignored on a Linear checkout gets a warning too.
- Tests: `server/start-card.test.ts` (parent ahead, merged, Done, `ask`/`base`, human Start again, explicit wins, `parent` without or with two parents, retry reproduces, `projects.update`), `server/agent-cards.test.ts` (create as follow-up, `ask`, explicit, outside caller, `BASE_INVALID`), `server/base-ref.test.ts` (real repo: branch and ahead, `changeBase` before and after the parent lands, squash), `server/landed-done.test.ts` (a follow-up lands like any card), `server/dispatcher.test.ts` (charter line, v4).
- Checks: see the card's progress note for the commit they ran on. Unrun: UI not opened in Paseo (installed plugin not reloaded, per the card).
- No tip, tour or What's new entry: the setting defaults to what the dispatcher was asked to do by hand, and the card line explains itself. No telemetry field.
- Review round 1 (independent review, fixes as new commits): the recorded `branchedFrom.commit` (and N) is now the new worktree's HEAD, read after the create on both paths (`actualBranchedFrom`); the rebase line is added at the card's first session also without a move (a `flowly_create_card` follow-up, or a retry after the session failed post-move, which also reports `base` and no "ignored" warning); the user's Start again with several parents ahead acts as `base` with a warning each; `worktreeBranch` reads the full `refs/heads/` name (a same-named tag no longer gives `heads/x`); an explicit parent branch that can't be compared refuses with `BASE_CHOICE_NEEDED`; a parent chosen explicitly with nothing ahead gives the project base with a warning. Spec §11.3 rules 1 and 3 and §11.4 updated. Deferred by the coordinator: a `flowly_create_card` retry recomputing a different base, and a same-key retry with a different `baseRef`.

## Step-throughs keep their place (branch `kanban/fix-stepping-through-unread-cards-the-n-31f164`) — 2026-11-16

- Audit of both step-throughs (only unread, only need-you, both, other groups, read or answered mid-walk, last card, archived/wrapped-up/Done cards, a card already open, chip count vs walk length, dispatcher waits, phone vs desktop, Previous, a new update mid-walk, repeated press) against DISPATCHER-SPEC §9.2/§9.4.
- Defect: since 868b909 (reading a session marks its card seen), every card the unread walk visited left the walk once you moved on, so the bar stayed at "1 of N" with N shrinking and ‹ Previous card was always disabled. Fix: the walk keeps the cards it visited (`stableQueue` `visited`, 6e27865).
- Ruling (Noah, 2026-11-16): the need-you walk holds its order like the unread walk; a card that newly needs you joins at the end, answered cards still drop out with "Answered" / Next card (`walkQueue`, 7278070).
- By spec, not changed: "N need you" counts asks while its walk counts cards, so a card with three asks reads "3 need you" and "1 of 1".
- Tests: `client/step-through.test.ts` (failing first: 1, then 4 of 30). Spec: DISPATCHER-SPEC §9.2 (walk order) and §9.4 (need-you order); the UI-REFRESH chip lines are unchanged.
- No tip, tour, What's new entry or telemetry field (a bug fix).

Step through them" label, step bar "Unread", "Snooze in Unread", help row "Step through unread cards", toast "Nothing unread." (old keys removed from all nine catalogs, with "Updates" and "Mark seen").
- Step bar: **Mark seen** and its plumbing (`afterMarkSeen`, the card/revision props) are gone; the "what changed" line and **Ask dispatcher** stay. Server unchanged: the change line, `dispatcher.mark-seen`, What should I do? and the clearing of a mark when a session is read.

## Merged cards move to Done from any open lane (branch `kanban/move-merged-cards-to-done-from-any-statu-eaa452`) — 2026-11-16

- Cause: `landStatuses` held only In review, Committed and Integrated (the first R2), so a card left in Development after its PR merged (PR #9229's card) was never a candidate: no move to Done and no dispatcher note.
- Fix: `landStatuses` is now Design, Development, Test, Blocked, In review, Committed and Integrated (`server/landed-done.ts`); the `stillLanding` re-check in `server/pr-review-archive.ts` uses the same list. PR review cards (§9, R11) are unchanged: In review only.
- Ruling (Noah, 2026-11-16, reverses the first R2): Todo and Backlog are skipped (no work to land), Done and Cancelled are finished. Only a session whose status is running or initializing skips a card, re-checked right before writing; idle, closed and errored sessions never block.
- Dispatcher finding: a system move to Done is an `isStop` status change, batched per card by NoteBatcher to the project's group; nothing to fix.
- PR lookup finding: the card's single PR output (§3.1), else for worktree cards `gh pr list --head` (§3.2), else the ancestor check (§3.3); paseo-linked-prs is deliberately not used.
- Tests added in `server/landed-done.test.ts` and `server/pr-review-archive.test.ts` (failing before the fix: 5 of 31, the four new landed-done/lane tests plus the `landCandidate` lane test; the existing "landed on origin/base" test timed out at 5 s on this machine regardless).
- Spec: LANDED-DONE-SPEC R2, R3, §3.1, §4, §8; AGENTS.md and SPEC-OUTLINE updated.
- Checks on 3d659c6 (rebased on main 02788d3): `npm run typecheck` exit 0; `npm test` exit 0 (225 files passed, 1 skipped; 2654 tests passed, 7 skipped). Unrun: no live check against PR #9229's card or a dispatcher (installed plugin not updated, per the card).
- No tip, tour, What's new entry or telemetry field (a bug fix; switch label unchanged).

## Dispatchers of different board groups message each other (branch `kanban/let-board-group-dispatchers-message-each-614595`) — 2026-11-16

- User rulings 2026-11-16 (DISPATCHER-SPEC §5.5): a new preapproved `flowly_message_dispatcher { groupId, text }`. It sends messages only; the receiver acts inside its own group, so `PROJECT_OUT_OF_GROUP` stays. `flowly_reply_to_agent` is unchanged. Messages need no confirm and show in both chats. Limits: 5 per target and 20 per hour per sender, plus a chain cap of 6 between two groups that resets when the user sends a message to either one. While the target waits on its user, messages go to a persistent queue. A group with no live dispatcher is refused with `DISPATCHER_DOWN`, and Flowly never starts one. The invariant that nothing about a card's asks crosses between dispatchers is in the spec and the charter.
- Finding the target: Flowly uses its own dispatcher record (`groups[groupId].agentId`), never Paseo's labels or titles, so an archived, pre-groups or replaced session in the shared workspace never receives a message. The card queue (`dispatcher-queue.json`) is keyed to card sessions, so it does not apply as is. The peer queue is a file of its own (`dispatcher-peer-queue.json`) that reuses the same file mechanics through a generic `JsonQueueFile`. The card queue's behavior and format are unchanged.
- The queue keeps messages while the target group's dispatcher is being started or replaced, and while it is unguarded; the next check delivers them to the new session. A message dropped right after it is queued returns `DISPATCHER_DOWN`, or `GROUP_NOT_FOUND` for a deleted group, and gives back its slot.
- The charter is now version 3 (cross-group rules and the invariant), so running dispatchers show the Replace warning. The guard version stays 2.
- Chat: the receiver shows a `From <group> dispatcher` row and collapses its answers the same way as answers to any agent. The sender shows a `To <group> dispatcher` row, only for calls that were sent or queued. Needs-you counts are unchanged. New strings ("To {name}", "{group} dispatcher") are in all catalogs.
- Files: `server/dispatcher-tools.ts`, `server/dispatcher-peer-queue.ts` (new), `server/dispatcher-queue.ts`, `server/dispatcher.ts` (`starting(groupId)`, `onUserMessage`, `onGroupDeleted`), `server/tool-server.ts`, `index.server.ts`, `shared/dispatcher.ts`, `shared/session-tail.ts`, `client/dispatcher-panel-logic.ts`, `client/session-state.ts`, `client/session-transcript.tsx`, `client/dispatcher-panel.tsx`, `client/board.tsx`, `shared/i18n/*`.
- Failing-then-passing tests: 6297915 and 7671023 (`server/dispatcher-tools.test.ts`, `server/dispatcher-peer-queue.test.ts`, `server/dispatcher.test.ts`, `shared/session-tail.test.ts`, `client/dispatcher-panel-logic.test.ts`). Review: opus-reviewer found no High findings and one Medium (queued messages dropped during a Replace), fixed in ba334b3. The re-review found nothing open.
- Unrun: a live exchange between two dispatchers in the app. The To row assumes the MCP tool call's input arrives as `detail.input`, which has not been seen in a real transcript yet. The installed plugin was not updated.
- No tip, tour or What's new entry: the feature is used by dispatchers, not started by the user. No telemetry field.

## Provider subagents in the Subagents chip, with a read-only transcript (branch `kanban/view-a-card-session-s-subagent-sessions-0a711f`) — 2026-11-16

- Spec: `docs/SUBAGENTS-SPEC.md` §9 (user-approved 2026-11-16) and the matching local-daemon exception in AGENTS.md. The coders and reviewers a card session starts with Claude Code's Agent tool (and Codex subagents) now show in the session view's Subagents chip next to its native children.
- Daemon: `server/provider-subagents.ts` (`ProviderSubagentService`) serves `subagents.provider-list` and `subagents.provider-tail` (`shared/rpc.ts`, registered in `index.server.ts`). Both check that the session is in the card's workspace, as `sessions.tail` does. Reads go over one reused local connection (`openLocalDaemon`): opened on first use (concurrent first calls share it), opened again after a drop, closed after 30 s without a call and on plugin stop. The list passes through only the spec's fields, strings capped, oldest first. The tail maps the daemon's `rows` (`seqStart`/`seqEnd` fall back to `seq`) through `slimTimeline`; a reset, stale cursor or gap returns `{ entries: [], reset: true }`. When the daemon is unavailable or this Paseo lacks the methods, the list returns `{ subagents: [], unavailable: true }` and the tail fails with a plain message; only unexpected errors go to `logFailure`, under a fixed label.
- Shared: `shared/subagents.ts` adds the descriptor schema, provider buckets (`running`, `failed`, everything else `done`), merged counts, `providerOrder` (oldest first, a nested subagent after its parent at depth 1; a cycle or a missing parent leaves it top-level) and `subagentRows` (native children first).
- Client: `useProviderSubagents` (`client/subagents.tsx`) reads the list while the session view is shown, settled and not archived, then every 5 s only while the session or a provider subagent runs; an empty result returns a shared constant. The chip's counts, segments and summary cover both kinds. A provider row (state dot, description or type, `state · subtitle`, nested rows indented) opens `client/subagent-transcript.tsx` over the session view, which stays mounted beneath it. That view has a header with Back, the title and `state · subtitle`, and renders the subagent's timeline with `SessionTranscript` itself (new `subagent` prop; `useSessionTimeline` reads `subagents.provider-tail`, newest page first, polls every 3 s only while it runs, reads once more when it stops; Load earlier, the phone FlatList, the empty state and the error notice are the transcript's own). Back, Esc and Android Back close it through a `stepBack` ref that the drawer checks before closing the side bar. Switching session (the view is keyed by session) or hiding or closing the side bar also closes it.
- Strings: `Completed`, `Failed` and `Back to session` are new in all 9 catalogs. The `subagents` tip text changed to "...open one to read its work.", the old string was removed from every catalog, and the `docs/ONBOARDING-SPEC.md` tip table was updated to match. No tour or What's new change (§9.5) and no telemetry field (a read-only view of existing data).
- Failing-then-passing tests (6cee36c, then 2ea229e): `shared/subagents.test.ts` (buckets, merged counts and segments, ordering with nesting, orphan and cycle, native-first rows, titles) and `server/provider-subagents.test.ts` (11 tests: field pass-through and caps, workspace and unknown-card refusal without connecting, unavailable and unsupported mapped to an empty list or a plain error with nothing logged, an unexpected error logged under a fixed label, slim page with `seq` fallback and paging, cursor required, reset/stale/gap, a daemon error message passed through, one shared connection with a 30 s idle close using fake timers and a close on stop, a dropped connection reopened, a failed open not cached).
- Checks on ea34435: `npm run typecheck` exit 0; `npm test` exit 0 (223 files passed, 1 skipped; 2616 tests passed, 7 skipped). A throwaway read-only run of `ProviderSubagentService` against the live daemon (Paseo 0.15.4, this card's session) listed both Agent-tool subagents and read a tail and a `before` page; an unknown subagent id failed with "Provider subagent not found". Unrun: desktop and phone UI checks (plugin not reinstalled, per the card; no screenshots), and no test of the client polling, the overlay or the Esc and Android Back step.
- Follow-up fixes: the client list read throws when the server reports `unavailable` (`providerListOrThrow` in `shared/subagents.ts`), so React Query keeps the last good list and `refetchInterval` keeps running (an open subagent no longer vanishes on a timeout or daemon restart); the tail's unsupported message is now "Update Paseo to view subagent conversations."; SUBAGENTS-SPEC §9.2 Failure rewritten to match the code.

## Reading a card's session clears it from "N updated" (branch `kanban/opening-a-card-s-session-should-clear-it-4f1551`) — 2026-11-16

- Cause: "N updated" (`updatedQueue`) is the dispatcher change line plus cards with an unread turn. Opening a card's unread session wrote a read receipt, so the green marker and the unread half dropped the card, but its change-line events stayed until an explicit Mark seen, so the chip kept counting it.
- Fix: `SessionReadStore.mark` reports when a read changed state; `SessionReadService.onRead` then calls `Dispatcher.cardRead(groupId, taskId)` (per-card `markSeen` up to now; skipped for a project without a group or a group without a dispatcher entry; failures logged with `logFailure`, never failing the read). `client/session-reads.ts` invalidates the dispatcher items after `markRead`, so the chip drops the card without waiting for the 4 s poll. It covers every read path (chat preview, drawer, step-through, phone) because they all go through `sessions.mark-read`. Spec: DISPATCHER-SPEC §9.2 ruling 2026-11-16; UI-REFRESH-SPEC chip line.
- Failing-then-passing tests (786cb09, 2 failed before dd86cdf): `server/dispatcher-items.test.ts` (card leaves the change line, group mark stays, other card stays, new event brings it back, group with no entry untouched), `server/session-reads.test.ts` (hook fires once on a changing read, not on a repeat read, a throwing hook does not fail the read), `client/step-through.test.ts` (queue length equals cards still unread/changed; a pure check, passed before too).
- Checks on dd86cdf + docs: `npm run typecheck` exit 0; `npm test` exit 0 (222 files passed, 1 skipped; 2599 tests passed, 7 skipped). Unrun: live desktop/phone UI check (installed plugin not updated, per the card); no test of the `index.server.ts` wiring or the client invalidation.
- No tip, tour, What's new entry or telemetry field (a bug fix).

## Move a card to Done when its work lands (branch `kanban/move-a-card-to-done-when-its-pr-merges-e31f26`) — 2026-11-16

- Spec: `docs/LANDED-DONE-SPEC.md` (rulings R1–R8, Noah 2026-11-16). The PR review archive check (`PrReviewArchiver`, `server/pr-review-archive.ts`) now builds one list of review cards (gated by *Archive review cards when the PR closes*) and ordinary cards in In review, Committed or Integrated (gated by the new *Move cards to Done when their work lands*, `doneOnLand` in the `pr-review-cards` record, on by default); one timer, one 20-lookup cap, least recently checked first; it runs when either switch is on.
- Evidence (`server/landed-done.ts`): exactly one distinct PR among the card's output artifacts (GitHub `/pull/<n>` URL or `external.itemType` `pull`) read through the connector, then `gh pr view`; else, for a worktree card with a `baseRef`, `gh pr list --head <branch>` in its directory, then the ancestor check (clean status, branch moved since its oldest reflog entry, `git fetch origin <base>`, `git merge-base --is-ancestor HEAD origin/<base>`). Argument arrays only; `gh`, `git` and the workspace lookup are injectable.
- Move to Done: inside one store transaction the card is read again (archived, lane, workspace, single PR); status, revision and a `status_changed` event with actor `system` and "Moved to Done: PR merged (owner/repo#n)" or "Moved to Done: work landed on <base>"; then the same daemon follow-up as a person's move (`afterStatus`: project memory review, guardrails finish, Auto wrap up). A PR closed without merging adds one `note` event "PR closed without merging: owner/repo#n", deduped against the card's events.
- Settings → Workflow → Board: new switch; 1 new string in all catalogs. The drawer's activity labels the new `note` action "Note" (existing string).
- Failing-then-passing tests: `server/landed-done.test.ts` (12 tests; 11 failed against a stub module, all pass now): merged PR output moves to Done and an open PR does nothing; a closed PR notes once, also after a restart; ancestor check moves a landed worktree card while fresh, dirty, unpushed and detached ones stay; a merged head PR moves a worktree card; a closed head PR gets no note; other lanes, busy, two PRs, input-only, directory and unreadable cards stay; each switch gates only its kind; one shared cap; re-check in the transaction; one log line for both kinds; PR output parsing; the `gh pr list` parser. `server/pr-review-archive.test.ts` unchanged and passing.
- Checks (Node 26.5.1): `npm run typecheck` exit 0; `npm test` exit 0 (218 files passed, 1 skipped; 2528 tests passed, 7 skipped).
- Review fixes (spec amended in c30f687): the ancestor check needs a `commit…` entry in the branch's reflog (`--format=%gs`), so a fresh branch fast-forwarded or reset to its base stays; a PR found by branch name counts only when not cross-repository and its `headRefOid` is the worktree's `HEAD`; a move re-reads that workspace's running sessions and requires the card's status and revision unchanged; the fetch uses `+refs/heads/<base>:refs/remotes/origin/<base>` with auto-gc and maintenance off, `GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT=0` and SSH in batch mode; `status` runs with `--no-optional-locks`; a base still starting with `refs/` gets no check. Failing-then-passing tests (6 failed before the fix): 4 new in `server/landed-done.test.ts` (fast-forward/reset stays, reused name or fork PR does not move, session started or revision bumped mid-check stays, fetch refspec/options and tag base), 2 updated (ancestor test's `gh` argument list, `gh pr list` parser). Checks: `npm run typecheck` exit 0; `npm test` exit 0 (218 files passed, 1 skipped; 2532 tests passed, 7 skipped). Second round (spec amended in 884f57d): step 2 reads the worktree's own HEAD reflog (`--format=%H%x09%gs HEAD`) and needs a `commit…` entry still an ancestor of HEAD (at most 20 tried), so a commit dropped by a reset or a reused branch's earlier commits no longer count; batch-mode SSH is added only when neither `GIT_SSH_COMMAND` nor `core.sshCommand` is set. Test repos are now real `git worktree add` worktrees; 2 new tests (reset-dropped and reused-branch cards stay; configured SSH command kept), 1 updated (reflog arguments), 3 failed before the fix. Checks: `npm run typecheck` exit 0; `npm test` exit 0 (218 files passed, 1 skipped; 2534 tests passed, 7 skipped).
- By spec (§4, 292d903): a move by this check asks no wrap-up question (that is a client prompt after the client's own move); `afterStatus` runs, so *Auto wrap up* applies, and the sidebar's Wrap up stays available. Unrun: live check against a real merged PR and the installed plugin.
- No tip or tour change, no telemetry field, no What's new entry (patch-level; not a release).

## Base branch for new worktrees (branch `kanban/branch-new-card-worktrees-off-a-configur-13f1e4`) — 2026-11-16

- Feature: project setting `baseRef` (project settings → *Base branch for new worktrees*, hidden for linked-root projects). `server/base-ref.ts` `projectWorktreeBase` picks the request's own ref, else the project's, else the main checkout's current branch; a remote-tracking base is fetched first (failure logged, last fetched ref used; a ref that never existed refuses), returned qualified, and `dropUpstream` removes any upstream from the new branch. Used by `flowly_start_card`, `BoardService.createTask` (New task, `flowly_create_card`) and an outside session's idea started as a new workspace; not by Switch to worktree (its copied diff is relative to the main checkout's HEAD). `flowly_start_card` now also stores the branch-off base on the card as `baseRef`, as New task already did, so the auto review diffs from it. START-CARD-SPEC §4.1.

## Dispatcher messages to a waiting card are queued, not refused (branch `kanban/don-t-lose-dispatcher-follow-ups-to-a-ca-c35bc7`) — 2026-11-16

- Cause: `flowly_message_card` refused with `SESSION_WAITING_ON_USER` while the card's session had a pending permission or AskUserQuestion, or an open question or blocker report, so a scope change sent then was lost and the user had to relay it by hand.
- Fix: the message is queued in `<dataDir>/dispatcher-queue.json` (`server/dispatcher-queue.ts`: `DispatcherQueueFile`, atomic and serialized, a corrupt file kept aside; `DispatcherQueue`) and the tool returns `{ sent: false, queued: true, sessionId, waitingOn, note }`. It counts toward `MESSAGE_LIMIT` when queued, never again on delivery; at most 10 per session (`QUEUE_FULL`). Delivery is checked on the session's turn start and end, permission resolution and archive (plugin hooks), right after queueing, and by a 60 s sweep while anything is queued; one session's messages go out as one steered message under one dispatcher marker, each with its time, telling the agent to re-read the card. Drops (card archived, Done, Cancelled or deleted; session archived, gone or moved) add a `dispatcher_message_dropped` event with the texts to the card's activity; a card's board change drops at once through the event bus. New RPCs `asks.queued` (etag) and `asks.cancel-question` (the user's "Cancel question and re-read card": denies the session's pending requests with `cancelledAskMessage`, then delivers). The banner (`client/queued-messages.tsx`) shows under the ask in the card sidebar's details, the session chat and the need-you step-through. Tool description and charter line updated; `dispatcherCharterVersion` and `dispatcherGuardVersion` stay 2 (the tool result carries the new behavior to running dispatchers; no tool added or withheld). DISPATCHER-SPEC §3, §5.3, §6, §8, §9.4, §10 (ruling, Noah, 2026-11-16).
- Failing-then-passing tests: `server/dispatcher-tools.test.ts` (queueing replaces the `SESSION_WAITING_ON_USER` test; `QUEUE_FULL`; delivery on ask end merged and once; report waits for the next turn, failed send kept; drops with activity for Done, Cancelled, archived, session archived and gone; Cancel question denies and delivers; survives a reload), `server/dispatcher-queue.test.ts` (delivery text, corrupt file kept), `server/dispatcher.test.ts` (charter line).
- Strings: "Scope changed since this was asked:", "Cancel question and re-read card", "Queued message sent", "Dropped a queued dispatcher message", in every catalog. Onboarding: no tour change (not a headline capability or a change to the Projects/Cards/Sessions map) and no tip (the banner shows in place, only when something is queued). Telemetry: no heartbeat field. What's new: none.
- Unrun: a live dispatcher and card session in Paseo (hook timing against a real daemon), and the UI on desktop and phone.
- Review round 1 fixes: the banner's queued list clears once the views stop reading it (a disabled query no longer shows its cached data); a message to a free session that still has older messages queued is queued behind them and checked at once, so order holds; every enqueue triggers a serialized check at once instead of waiting for the 60 s sweep; a corrupt queue file that can't be renamed aside refuses reads and writes (`QUEUE_FAILED`) instead of starting empty over it. Failing-then-passing tests in `server/dispatcher-tools.test.ts` (order behind a backlog; ask ended between read and write) and `server/dispatcher-queue.test.ts` (file that can't be kept aside).
- Rulings (Noah, 2026-11-16), recorded in DISPATCHER-SPEC §5.3 rulings (4) and (5): Cancel question and re-read card denies only the request its banner sits on (`asks.cancel-question` takes an optional `requestId`; step-through rows pass their own, the sidebar and session chat banners the session's oldest pending request, report banners none) and delivers only when no other request of the session is pending, else the queue waits for those (new toast "The message waits for the session's other requests", in every catalog); queued messages are dropped with an activity event when the card's project is archived (`cardEnded` checks the project; `projects.update` calls `DispatcherQueue.projectChanged` so the drop is immediate; the sweep catches other paths). AGENTS.md's dispatcher clause now says a message to a waiting session is queued and delivered after the user resolves the ask. Failing-then-passing tests in `server/dispatcher-tools.test.ts` (Cancel denies only the pressed request; project archive drops).
- Review round 2 fixes: the card sidebar's and session chat's session-wide banners pass a request to deny only when it is the session's one pending request (`bannerRequestId`, `client/queued-request.ts`), so a press never denies a request the user did not press on; `flowly_message_card` checks the session right after the queue write and reports what happened to its message (`DispatcherQueue.outcome`): delivered is `{ sent: true }`, dropped is `{ sent: false, queued: false, dropped: true, reason, note }`, else queued; the delivery header says the messages were held until the session could take them, true for a backlog too. Failing-then-passing tests: `client/queued-request.test.ts`, `server/dispatcher-tools.test.ts` (dropped at once is reported as dropped), `server/dispatcher-queue.test.ts` (header).

## Ship to main no longer loops on a stopped ship (branch `kanban/stop-the-auto-review-ship-loop-and-check-85c152`) — 2026-11-14

- Cause: every agent delivery to In review starts an auto review (`AutoReviewService.review`), and every pass on a `ship_main` card sent the ship message, with no memory of an earlier ship at the same commit. A ship that stopped on a rebase conflict re-delivered at the same head, passed again, got the same ship message and hit the same conflict (seen on card 9317e741, 2026-11-14).

New commit, a person's move or Rerun lifts it. DELIVERY-MODE-SPEC §4.1, AUTO-REVIEW-SPEC §3.1.
- Failing-then-passing test: `server/auto-review.test.ts` ("a ship that stopped is not reviewed or shipped again at the same head…"; before the fix it got a second ship message).

One earlier run hit an `ENOTEMPTY` temp-dir cleanup race in an unrelated `auto-review.test.ts` afterEach; three reruns of the file and the full rerun passed. No UI string, tip, telemetry field or What's new change.

## Updated picker: per-card Mark seen, stable walk, change summaries, no echoes of the user's own requests (branch `kanban/fix-the-updated-picker-wrong-mark-seen-u-bffc04`) — 2026-11-12

- DISPATCHER-SPEC §9.2 ruling (2026-11-12). Mark seen: `dispatcher.mark-seen` gains `taskId`; per-card marks in `seenCards` of the group entry; `changeLine` counts an event only after both the group mark and the card's mark; the picker marks the shown card and moves to the next one (`afterMarkSeen`), ending when none is left.
- Cause of the jumping position (found by reading the data flow, shown by a test): the walk used the live queue, recomputed on every poll; opening a card reads its turn and drops it, and any new event moved a card to the front, so the neighbours of the card shown changed. Fix: `stableQueue` keeps the walk's own order.
- Change summaries: `ChangedCard.changes` (`changeOf` in `server/dispatcher-items.ts`), shown by `changeSummary` as one line on the picker. 7 new strings in all catalogs ("Moved to {lane}" already existed).
- Echoes: on a card the dispatcher created, the card session's events up to its first stop are not news (`echoes`, `isStopEvent` now shared from `server/event-bus.ts`). The product rule is the narrowest reading and awaits Noah's confirmation; the cases left uncovered are listed in the spec and in the card's progress note.
- Failing-then-passing tests: `server/dispatcher-items.test.ts` (per-card mark, summaries, echoes), `client/step-through.test.ts` (stable walk, after Mark seen, summary text).
- Checks: `npm run typecheck` 0; `npm test` 214 files passed, 1 skipped; 2421 tests passed, 7 skipped. UNRUN: live UI on desktop and phone.
- Onboarding: no tip or tour change (a fix to an existing flow). Telemetry: no field. What's new: none.

## A started card leaves Backlog/Todo (branch `kanban/starting-a-card-moves-it-out-of-backlog-5a564c`) — 2026-11-12

- Cause (verified in the board's event log, not assumed): the eight "Move Loadout memory into…" cards had sessions that wrote tags, workpad, memory and a progress report but never called `flowly_update_status`; no `status_changed` event and no refusal (directory workspaces are not refused) exists for any of them, so nothing ever moved them out of Backlog.
- Fix: starting a card moves it from Backlog/Todo to the active-work lane by role (`startedStatus` in `shared/lanes.ts`: Development, else first enabled work lane; none leaves it). `server/start-stage.ts` `moveStartedCard` runs after `flowly_start_card` creates the session and in the `prepareSession` RPC for the board's starts. Other statuses, archived cards and Blocked/Done/Cancelled stay. Spec: DISPATCHER-SPEC §4.1, START-CARD-SPEC §5.
- Tests: `server/start-card.test.ts` (Backlog, Todo, Development/Test/In review/Blocked unchanged, lane chosen by role, failed start leaves the card), `server/start-stage.test.ts` (board path, all statuses, archived, lane choice); red first (3 failing), then green. Two older start-card assertions now expect the extra move (revision 3, last `workspace_switched` event looked up by action).
- Checks: `npm run typecheck` exit 0; `npm test` 215 files passed, 2433 tests passed, 7 skipped. Not run: live board/UI check of the drawer Start session.
- Onboarding upkeep: no tour or tip change (behavior fix, no new control).

## New session form: default model no longer offered is named; starting text per project (branch `session-form-defaults`) — 2026-11-12

Choose another default in Settings → Models." MODEL-DEFAULTS-SPEC §2. Noah still needs to choose a new default in Settings → Models.

Project setting `sessionStartText` (Project settings → New session starting text), built-in default "Read the card and pick up where it left off."; the New session box opens with it (an idea's own prompt wins); an empty box sends the card's title and description. UI-REFRESH-SPEC §7s marked superseded for New session. Note: the title and description are already in the session's system prompt, so an empty box repeats them as the first message (the duplication §7s removed); kept as requested.

- Ruling (Noah, 2026-11-10, relayed): Insights, Ideas and Memory (and I, B, M) work on an unfiltered group board over every live member; filtered, over that project. Guardrails stays per project. Spec §0 ruling, §4, §8.
- `pageScope` / `addProject` (`client/board-groups.ts`); Ideas: `useProjectsIdeasQuery` (one etag read per project), `groupByCard` keeps one own-ideas group per project, project labels, Import asks which project (`client/project-choice.tsx`), `groupOpenIdeaCount`. Memory: `useGuardrailsQueries`, entries labelled by project, edits on the entry's project, read-only for archived or guardrails-off members, Add memory asks which project. Insights: the group reads of ecc2d83 (`insights.group*`) wired in the client; the day's summary, Skills and Other suggestions per project, labelled. board-view `lastAddProjectId`.
- Last group and filter across restarts: board-view `lastGroupId` and `lastFilter`; `pickGroupView(..., stored)` falls back to the default group when the stored one is gone; `restoredFilter` (this run's choice wins); both are stored only once the board and the settings have loaded. Spec §3, §4, §7 step 5.
- Merge of origin/main: the slim card menu (`client/card-menu.ts`) gains `groupOrder` (Move up / Move down on a group board's phone sheet, scope group unfiltered). The slim-menu change on main had dropped the Add group / Delete group dialogs from the render along with the old action sheet; they are kept here (main still lacks them until this merges).

Telemetry: no field. Strings: 2 new ("Skills · {project}", the guardrails-off notice for several projects) in all catalogs.
- Lanes review follow-ups: none open (all in the entry below).
- Fix round 1 (rulings, Noah 2026-11-11, relayed): the bell, N/U and "N need you" cover the shown group or filtered project; other groups' need-you counts show on the group tabs and switcher rows (`otherGroupNeeds`, `needLabel`), the sidebar badge stays global. Group pages cover live members only (the archived read-only branches are gone). Last group and filter are per host. New project filters the board to it (`focusProject(..., pin)`). Group settings Discard resets every dirty section (`afterDiscard`, a discard epoch reseeds project sections); Add group there asks about unsaved edits first.
- Fix round 2: board-view saves keep stored keys the schema does not know (`saveMerged` spreads the raw values under the parsed ones), so the v1 `boardGroups` the migration reads survives a preference save. A dispatcher write checks the caller's group again inside its own transaction (`checkGroup`, `CardCaller.groupId`: card create and update, idea propose and update, link, archive; start at the run, the worktree rebind and before the session), `PROJECT_OUT_OF_GROUP`. DISPATCHER-SPEC §4.
- Fix round 1 bugs: the tab menu's New task opens in that tab's group (`groupNewTask`); a cold card link into the default group keeps its card (the focus always names the group and records this run's filter); a stale stored filter restored after the board loaded is cleared (effect deps; no unit test, it needs a render). Spec: Done count follows the group's Done lane, archiving the filtered project clears the filter, a card of an archived project leaves the board unfiltered, Phones subsection in §4; superseded rulings folded into the ones they change.

## Group boards: one manual order per column (branch `group-needs-you`) — 2026-11-10

- Ruling (Noah, 2026-11-10): Manual sort on a group board is one order per column across the group's projects, reorderable by drag and Move up/down, filtered or not; the filtered board shows that order restricted to the project. BOARD-GROUPS-SPEC §0, §1, §2, §4, §7 step 8 and §8; ALL-PROJECTS-SPEC's superseded note.
- Model: the card's one `position` is comparable across a group; `project.orderGroupId` (server-kept) marks which group's order a project's positions are in. `shared/group-order.ts`: `syncGroupOrder` (a project not yet in its group's order joins the end of each column in its own order: live first, then archived, in chooser order), `orderScope`, `columnEnd`, `neighborCard`. The store runs it in the start migration (same write as groups and lanes, no revision bump; on existing data it reproduces the old grouped-by-project display), before each operation and at the end of each commit (moves by set-group, set_members, group delete, the repair; new and folder-restored projects), and on each read's copy.
- `tasks.place` anchors on any card of the card's group once groups are ready (another group: "Drop position changed"); `tasks.move` gains `scope: "group" | "project"`; new cards and PR-review lane moves go after the group column's last card. Status changes keep `position`, as per project before. The project folder copy omits `orderGroupId`.
- Client: a group board's Manual sorts by position across projects (`sortAllColumn(..., groupOrder)`), `reorderAllowed` allows it with Manual on a group board, the card menu and phone action sheet show Move up/down there (scope `group` unfiltered, `project` filtered). A board shown before its group is known keeps the old rules.
- Onboarding: no tip or tour change (drag reorder and Move up/down already exist on project boards; this lifts a limit rather than adding a moment worth pointing out). What's new: none (no release). Telemetry: no field. Strings: none.

## A card's plan: daemon and tools (branch `card-plan-slot`) — 2026-11-10

- CARD-PLAN-SPEC, server and shared half; the client (Plan row, Plan role in the artifact form, Start session defaults, activity labels, tour, tip, strings) is a separate step.
- `Artifact.plan` (optional; board version unchanged). `shared/plans.ts`: `planOf`, `planApproved`, `withoutPlan`, `planContextLine`, `suggestPlanAwareMode`, `planRequestFacts`, `planTitle`. `server/plans.ts`: `setPlan`, `approvePlan`, `clearPlan` (one plan per card, review ledgers and external items refused, events `plan_set` / `plan_approved` / `plan_cleared`, board revision only), `writePlanCopy`, `cardPlanView`, `PlanModeWatcher` (wired to `agent.permission.before` / `.after` for managed card sessions; never blocks or throws) and `PlanService` (RPCs `plans.set`, `plans.approve`, `plans.clear`).
- Artifact roots take the plugin data folder: `artifactRoots(..., dataDirectory)` adds the card's own `plans/<taskId>` (ArtifactService.where, AgentService's three sites, get_task's missing check). Deleting the plan artifact records `plan_cleared`; an agent's `flowly_remove_artifact` on it is `ARTIFACT_FORBIDDEN`.
- Tools: `flowly_set_plan`, `flowly_clear_plan` on the card and board servers (and CLI `set-plan`, `clear-plan`); `flowly_create_card.plan` (checked before anything is made; the dispatcher gets it through the shared schema); `flowly_get_task.cardPlan` (named apart from the workpad's `plan`); `flowly_get_card` card `plan`; session context `Plan (approved|draft): …` before Inputs; skill line. `flowly_start_card.mode` (`MODE_UNAVAILABLE`), `suggestPlanAwareMode` after the New task defaults, default prompt with the approved plan.
- Spec changes made while implementing: `cardPlan` instead of `plan` in get_task, external items refused with their own message, a create_card plan's relative path is relative to the project root, mode checked against the pre-start directory, plan-copy name suffixes, CLI commands.
- Tests: `shared/plans.test.ts`, `server/plans.test.ts`, additions to `server/artifacts.test.ts`, `server/agent-cards.test.ts`, `server/tool-server.test.ts`, `server/start-card.test.ts`. No strings added (agent and daemon text stays English). Onboarding, What's new and telemetry: left to the client step (tip `plan`, More features entry); no heartbeat field.
- Checks: `npm run typecheck` exit 0; `npm test` exit 0 (208 files passed, 1 skipped; 2290 passed, 7 skipped). Unrun: plan-mode capture against a live Paseo session (covered by unit tests only).
- Review fix round (independent review of 956b68c..da846d7): a directory card's plan is stored absolute (a `./` plan went Missing after `flowly_start_card` moved the card to a worktree), on every writer through `planTarget`; re-approving the approved plan's target renews the approval and records `plan_set`; plan mode prefers the user's `updatedInput` text and path; the before hook keeps a plan request at once and the answer waits up to 10 s for the session lookup (a fast allow was lost); a later link to the plan's URL never makes it external; `flowly_set_plan` reports the real `missing`; plan mode checks the card is live before writing a copy. Test added: a card's roots exclude another card's plan copies.
- Review fix round 2 (on 12c48eb): `putArtifact` treats a `./x` file and its absolute form in the workspace as one target (`sameTarget`, also used by `setPlan`), so re-adding a directory card's plan by its relative path no longer makes a second record; text the user edited at plan approval becomes a plan copy even when the request named a file; the plan watcher logs a session-lookup timeout and reads the approval inside its try; an agent's `approved: true` over an approval by a person or plan mode is a no-op. Spec §3.1 notes the worktree-to-local `./` plan limit.

Git facts come from the workspace descriptor, `git` (argument arrays) only fills gaps.

The `<owner>-<repo>` folder layout's name collisions are noted in the spec, not changed. One case could not be tested: two live cards bound to the same workspace, which the board's invariant forbids. The shared-folder case is tested instead.

## Board groups v2, 6/7: flowly_manage_groups (confirmed) — 2026-11-10

- Task 6 of the board groups v2 plan (DISPATCHER-SPEC §0, §5.2; BOARD-GROUPS-SPEC §2). `flowly_manage_groups { action: create | rename | set_members | set_default | delete, groupId?, name?, projectIds?, revision? }` is in `dispatcherConfirmedToolNames` only: never preapproved, never in `savedTools`.
- Same checks and order as `flowly_create_project` (caller, switch, Paseo, `CONFIRMATION_UNAVAILABLE` in non-prompting modes); its own bucket of 10 per hour (`GROUP_LIMIT`), released on refusal. The mode check is shared (`assertPrompting`).
- `set_members` is one store write (`setGroupMembers`): listed projects move in, a left-out member goes to the default group, the default group only adds (`DEFAULT_REQUIRED`), an archived non-member is refused (`PROJECT_ARCHIVED`); the group revision is bumped when a member moves; moved projects get `projectMoved`.
- `delete` refuses the caller's own group (`GROUP_PROTECTED`) and runs `Dispatcher.deleteGroup` with `refuseLive`: a live session (running, idle, closed) is `GROUP_BUSY`, decided inside the start queue; a dead one is not archived and the group goes.
- Wiring: `server/group-writes.ts` (`groupWriter`) is the one set of group writes; index.server wires it into the tool. Charter: manage groups only when the user asks; each call is confirmed.
- Tests: `server/dispatcher-tools.test.ts` (confirmed, not preapproved, mode and switch order, bucket and refusal slot, create/rename/default rules, each refusal, `set_members` semantics and atomicity).

## Board groups v2, 5/7: one dispatcher per group — 2026-11-10

- Task 5 of the board groups v2 plan (DISPATCHER-SPEC, BOARD-GROUPS-SPEC §6, §7 step 4). DISPATCHER-SPEC's §0 list is folded into §1–§10 and deleted.
- Record v2 (`groups` map, global `previousAgentIds` capped at 100, `unassigned`, `legacyGroupId`) with the one-time v1 migration (session to the group with the most open cards, ties first group, independent of projects, run after the board's group migration, after a storage restore and lazily); v1 and v2 files are read, an unknown or invalid file is preserved.
- `Dispatcher` is the shared hub (record, workspace, one queue for start, replace and group delete); each group has a lazily created `GroupDispatcher` (held notes, cooldown, timers, start window, its own note batcher). `status`, `start`, `replace` and every `dispatcher.*` RPC take `groupId`. Title `Flowly dispatcher · <group>`, labels `flowly-dispatcher-group`, model chain group → host → default (`effectiveDispatcherModel`).
- Credential: optional `groupId` in the sealed identity, the group found from the record entry holding the token's `agentId`; `PROJECT_OUT_OF_GROUP` before the project checks on every call; `flowly_list_projects` and `flowly_create_project` follow the caller's group; the hourly buckets stay per session.
- Notes: the group is stamped on the fact when the stop is built and each group has its own batcher; `exclude` covers every id ever recorded; held notes (batcher buffer and held map) move on `projectMoved` and are dropped when the target has no dispatcher. Chat notes are stamped with `groupId` and `agentId` and a chat shows only its own session's notes (ruling, Noah 2026-11-10: this replaced the BOARD-GROUPS-SPEC wording "filtered by the project's current group", now corrected); cap and pruning per key; change line, cache and seen mark per group.
- `groups.delete` runs on the dispatcher queue: store pre-checks, refuse while running, archive, record, board; `DELETE_FAILED` on a failed archive; a late start writes nothing back. The interim v1 guard (`server/group-delete-guard.ts`) is gone.
- Client: the dock follows the shown project's group, else the default group (`groups` is now part of the board overview); the panel copy is group wording in all catalogs. Group tabs and the switcher are task 3.
- Onboarding, What's new and telemetry: none (one copy line; no switch or heartbeat field).

## Dispatcher messages a card's session (`flowly_message_card`) — 2026-11-10

- `flowly_message_card { projectId, taskId, text, sessionId? }` (DISPATCHER-SPEC §5.3): a steered, prefixed plain instruction to a card's session. Default session: the only live one, or the only running one; else `SESSION_AMBIGUOUS`. Refused with `SESSION_WAITING_ON_USER` on a pending permission or question in Paseo's snapshot or an open question or blocker report; it never answers anything. ## Board groups v2, 1/7: groups in board data, invariants, RPCs, migration — 2026-11-10

- Task 1 of the board groups v2 plan (BOARD-GROUPS-SPEC §1, §2, §7 steps 1, 2, 5, 6). No UI change: the client still reads the v1 `board-view` fields; dropping them from the settings parser is task 3.
- Model: `board.groups` (`.default([])`, no version bump; a generic `groups` collection in SQLite), `project.groupId`, project `deliveryMode` override (`projects.update`, null clears), group input schemas. Pure rules in `shared/groups.ts`: name helpers, `migrateLegacyGroups` (§7 steps 1–2), `ensureGroups` (the §1 repair), `dispatcherTarget` (§7 step 4's choice), `assertGroupInvariants` (on commit only).
- Store: `migrateGroups` is queued by the plugin entry right after the store is built, before every other read or write; it migrates the host's v1 groups (raw parse in `server/legacy-board-groups.ts`, the settings file is never written), then repairs, in one write; a second start writes nothing. Until it has run, group writes refuse with `GROUPS_UNAVAILABLE` and no write adds groups; a failure writes nothing and is logged. After it, every commit ends with the repair and the invariants, so new, restored and unarchived projects are grouped; reads repair their copy only. A storage restore migrates again. Folder copies (`.flowly/project.json`) carry no `groupId`.
- RPCs: `groups.create/update/move/delete`, `projects.set-group`, `projects.create` `groupId` (an unknown group is refused before Paseo registers anything), `groups` in board patches. Refusals are coded (`CODE: message`). `groups.delete` refuses the group the v1 dispatcher would move to while a session is stored (ruling, Noah 2026-11-10; `server/group-delete-guard.ts`, replaced by task 5).
- Rulings (Noah, 2026-11-10), in the spec's §0: a stored "Projects" is reused as the default; a new `Projects` counts toward the 50-group cap; blank names drop the group; `isDefault: false` on a non-default group is a no-op. Decided here: an unreadable (not missing) settings file fails the migration.
- Review fix round (opus-reviewer of 4c169b6): a storage-busy migration failure retries with backoff during the run; restore and export move group revisions too; the repair also fixes repeated group ids and names (logged); a no-op `groups.update` bumps nothing; an unreadable dispatcher record refuses a group delete with `DISPATCHER_UNAVAILABLE` (real error logged). New tests: retry, non-retryable no retry, restore with groups and a stale group edit, broken stored list, no-op update, unreadable record, failed JSON save during the migration, repaired read and next write agreeing on the default id, `GROUP_BUSY` through the plugin entry.
- Tests: `shared/groups.test.ts` (every §8 migration fixture, repair, invariants, `dispatcherTarget`), `server/store-groups.test.ts` (JSON and SQLite: migration, ordering before queued writes, failed write at `save:written`, second start, every RPC refusal, guard, folder copy, restore), `server/legacy-board-groups.test.ts`, a plugin-entry check of the RPC wiring and coded refusals.
- Onboarding, What's new and telemetry: none (no UI, switch or heartbeat field). Strings: none.
## Archive PR review cards when their PR closes; `flowly_archive_card` (branch `auto-archive-review-cards`) — 2026-11-10

- `server/pr-review-archive.ts` (PR-REVIEW-CARDS-SPEC §10): `PrReviewArchiver`, a timer (first check after 60 s, then every 10 min, `unref`, stopped on unload) that archives PR review cards in In review with a `prRef` once their PR is merged or closed. Lookups: the GitHub connector (`ConnectorService.summaries`, github.com connections without `authFailed`) then `gh pr view` through `execFile`; at most 20 per check, least recently checked first; cards in workspaces with a running session are skipped; a check where no lookup works logs once. The archive is a store transaction mirroring `editTask`'s archive (actor `system`, note "Archived: PR merged/closed"), rechecked inside the write. The card's workspace is never archived: PR review cards never own theirs, and archiving it would go past the "archiving plugin records doesn't archive Paseo resources" invariant.
- New host setting *Archive review cards when the PR closes* (`archiveOnClose` in the `pr-review-cards` record, on by default), next to *Make cards for PR review agents*; 1 new string in all catalogs.
- `flowly_archive_card { projectId, taskId, archived = true, reason? }` (DISPATCHER-SPEC §5), dispatcher only, preapproved (in `dispatcherToolNames`, so a running dispatcher shows the new-tools notice until replaced). `flowly_list_cards` cards carry `prRef` when set (card sessions see it too). Charter: archive only when the user asks. The dispatcher test's name guard (`/replace|archive|dispatcher/`) was narrowed to `/replace|dispatcher|archive_(session|agent|workspace)/`: the ruling it guards is about archiving the dispatcher, not cards.
- Onboarding: no tour change or tip (a background tidy-up with a default-on switch; existing users see no new flow). Telemetry: no heartbeat field.

## Dispatcher creates projects, confirmed by the user (branch `feat/dispatcher-project-tools`) — 2026-11-10

- `flowly_create_project { path, title?, description? }` → `{ projectId, title, path }` (DISPATCHER-SPEC §5.2): a single-directory project from an existing folder through the `projects.create` RPC's own function (`createProject` in `index.server.ts`, handed to `DispatcherTools.createProjectWith`): the same board service call, dispatcher-folder refusal, native registration, duplicate refusal and guardrail command detection. Default title: the folder's name. Refusals of that path are `PROJECT_REFUSED` with the RPC's message; the caller check and `AGENT_CARDS_DISABLED` come first. Linked multi-repo creation and restoring a folder's saved `.flowly/` project are not offered.
- Ruling (Noah, 2026-11-10): the project tool is not preapproved. `dispatcherConfirmedToolNames` sits beside `dispatcherToolNames` (the preapproved list, which is also what `savedTools` records and `missesTools` checks), so the new tool prompts in the dispatcher chat and never raises the new-tools or unguarded warning; a dispatcher started before it lists it from its server and prompts too.
- Charter: create a project only when the user asks, from the folder they name; each call is confirmed; on `PROJECT_REFUSED` tell the user.
- Review fix round (Opus review of c0aed52; rulings, Noah 2026-11-10, DISPATCHER-SPEC §5.2): `flowly_create_project` refuses with `CONFIRMATION_UNAVAILABLE` while the dispatcher's `currentModeId` (Paseo's agent snapshot) is `bypassPermissions`, `auto` or `dontAsk`; at most 5 creations per dispatcher hour (`PROJECT_LIMIT`, its own bucket, refusals give the slot back); on the dispatcher's path a root inside, equal to or holding the plugin data folder or `<Paseo home>/flowly-dispatcher` is refused on canonical paths (the form's RPC unchanged). Relative paths are invalid input; `~` alone is home; the path is no longer trimmed; a blank folder name gets the title `Project`, and every refusal after the input check is `PROJECT_REFUSED`. The RPC and the tool share `projectCreator` (`server/project-create.ts`); tests now go through it (guardrail detection, one queue with the form) and cover the unwired, mode, cap and reserved-folder refusals. Unenforceable, documented: settings allow rules and a client's "always allow". Checks after the fix round: `npm run typecheck` exit 0; `npm test` exit 0 (193 files passed, 1 skipped; 1965 passed, 7 skipped).
- Second review fixes (re-review of 15a672d): the mode check is an allowlist, `default`, `acceptEdits` and `plan` (Paseo's Claude mode ids); any other mode, an unknown id and no mode reported are `CONFIRMATION_UNAVAILABLE` (`dontAsk`, not a Paseo id, dropped). `server/plugin-entry.test.ts` runs the real plugin entry against a fake host and checks that `flowly_create_project`'s `createProjectWith` and the `projects.create` handler reach the same board service and guardrail detection; with the wiring line removed it fails (checked once by hand). Checks: `npm run typecheck` exit 0; `npm test` exit 0 (194 files passed, 1 skipped; 1966 passed, 7 skipped).
- Board groups: no dispatcher tool (ruling C). Groups move into board data on a later card, which will give the dispatcher a group tool without a local-daemon exception.
  - Checks: `npm run typecheck` exit 0; `npm test` exit 0 (193 files passed, 1 skipped; 1961 passed, 7 skipped). A first full run had one failure in `server/auto-review.test.ts` (ENOTEMPTY removing its temp folder; that file passes alone and in the rerun; not touched here). Unrun: a live dispatcher showing the permission prompt for `flowly_create_project` and creating a project (no plugin reload in this task).

## Board groups: lanes are group-level (branch `group-needs-you`) — 2026-11-10

- Ruling (Noah, 2026-11-10), reversing "lanes stay per project": a group owns its lanes; BOARD-GROUPS-SPEC §0, §1, §2, §4, §5, §7 step 7 and §8 rewritten.
- Model: `Group.lanes` (optional only for data stored before it) and `groups.update { lanes }`. Each member's `project.lanes` is a store-kept mirror, so the ~25 readers of a project's lanes (Move to, start status, auto status, agent and dispatcher tools, workpad, auto review, insights, summaries, PR review cards, quick task, drawer, ideas) resolve through the group unchanged.
- `shared/group-lanes.ts`: `groupLanesFrom` (the migration union: statuses on in any live member or holding a live card of one, default order, a name kept only when every member using the lane agrees, all lanes for an empty group), `groupLaneCounts`, and `syncGroupLanes` (fill a missing group's lanes, turn on statuses holding live cards of live members and bump that group's revision, mirror to every member). The store runs it in the start migration (same write as the groups migration, so a second start writes nothing), at the end of every commit after the group repair, and on each read's copy. Project moves (set-group, set_members, group delete, the repair), project create, unarchive and folder restore are thus covered by one path.
- Refusals: `groups.update` turning off a lane with live cards of live members is `LANE_NOT_EMPTY` ("Move the cards out of {lane} (n) before turning it off."); `projects.update` with `lanes` is `LANES_IN_GROUP`. New groups get every lane.
- Client: the group board's columns and names are the group's, filtered or not (`boardLanes`); `dropAllowed` takes the group, so a member's card may drop on any column. The lane editor left the project fields (Edit project, the group page's project sections) and sits on the group settings page with Cancel/Save; its counts are over the group's live members, and unsaved lane edits join the discard question.
- Decided here (the ruling did not say): cards of archived projects neither count for the union nor block turning a lane off (a restore turns their lanes back on); the migration bumps no revision; the safety net turning on a lane for a live card applies to every write, not only project moves; the group's lane editor keeps unsaved edits across revision bumps that leave the lanes as they were, reseeds an untouched editor when the lanes change elsewhere, and refuses the first Save of an edited one (`client/group-lanes-logic.ts`).
- Tests (written first, failed before the change): `shared/group-lanes.test.ts`, `server/store-group-lanes.test.ts` (JSON and SQLite: migration, idempotence, mirror after lane edit, `LANE_NOT_EMPTY`, `LANES_IN_GROUP`, editTask/placeTask/createTask refused on a group-off lane, set-group, set_members, delete, project create, invariant check after each), `client/all-projects.test.ts` (group columns and names filtered and not, drops). Changed: `server/project-order.test.ts`'s per-project lane test now asserts `LANES_IN_GROUP` (its turn-off and refusal assertions moved to the group test); `server/agent-service.test.ts` sets lanes through the group.
- Checks: `npm run typecheck` exit 0; `npm test` exit 0 (202 files passed, 1 skipped; 2206 passed, 7 skipped), on the final tree. Unrun: a live click-through of the group lane editor and of a migrated board (no plugin reload in this task).
- Review fix round (independent review of 6b6a83c): the migration keeps the members' lane order when every live member's full lane list has the same order (one member keeps its own), else the default order; reads fill in and mirror lanes without bumping any revision (`syncGroupLanes` `bumpRevision: false` in `repaired()`), so an edit at the stored revision still succeeds; the group lane editor is keyed on the group only and keeps its edits across revision bumps that leave the lanes unchanged, reloads when untouched, and refuses one Save after the lanes changed elsewhere ("Lanes changed elsewhere. Your edits were kept; review and save again.", new in all 9 catalogs; the second Save overwrites). Pure parts in `client/group-lanes-logic.ts`. Tests (failed first): order cases and the no-bump option in `shared/group-lanes.test.ts`, the read case in `server/store-group-lanes.test.ts` (JSON), `client/group-lanes-logic.test.ts`. Spec: §7 step 7 order rule, §1 reads, §5 editor conflict.
- Onboarding: no tour change or tip (the tour does not mention lanes). What's new: one 1.9.0 item (lanes moved to Group settings, an entry point existing users would not find). Strings: the lane editor's help sentence now says group (replaced in all 9 catalogs) and the What's new sentence is new in all 9. Telemetry: no field (off in this fork).

The workspace part is skipped when another live card shares the workspace; sessions bound to another card, archived ones and helpers (`flowly: summarizer` / legacy `ultra-kanban: summarizer`, `flowly-dispatcher`, Paseo `internal`) never count. The server error now reads "This card has no chats to summarize yet."
