## 2026-10-24 Limited animation smoothness optimization

Wrote the [MOBILE-SMOOTHNESS-SPEC](MOBILE-SMOOTHNESS-SPEC.md) first, comparing the dot-matrix at rest, the shared-lease candidate and a reverse baseline against the frozen source on the Samsung. Only the new dot-matrix clock reuses the height lease, and the grid is kept; an independent mounted run reproduced one extra stop/start during reversal, which v2 removes with only a microtask re-check of the existing guard, with no new timer, state or framework. Continuous 220/160ms, fresh, 450ms and the unified width are kept. Final collapse is 26.13/28.21ms (was 39.28/39.62), expand is still 59.07/25.15ms, and the 40–48ms segment and the unconfirmed end state from Idea 125ms on are both kept; this is not an overall FPS gain. Of 41 windows, 37 are valid and 4 excluded; the old 140 captures / 37 windows passed the independent Luna audit (PASS), and the final 161 captures / 41 windows passed the parent recomputation (PASS); the independent audit of the 4 new windows is UNRUN. Earlier, on the shared dirty tree, V2 typecheck, Luna mounted 8 / root focused 22 / full 125 files with 1024 tests PASS, and the hashes of 374 sources were stable. A normal plugin reload and the Samsung being ready, and cleanup of owned entries/runtime/tools/sessions PASS; 17 MiB of compressed evidence is kept, and other sessions' work and the shared cache are kept; the user's later authorization to ship covers only this fix and the spec, and the isolated tree is accepted separately. See the [record of this round](MOBILE-SMOOTHNESS-SPEC.md#6-execution-results-and-bounds).

## 2026-10-24 Wide-screen dot-matrix continuity fix

The repeated replay when cards are opened in succession is the visible cost of phase 0 being restored after the global height yield. Per MOBILE-SMOOTHNESS-SPEC §7, the decorative clock yields only when the host window is <760; on wide screens the same loop/phase is kept, and lease and resize notifications do not restart it. Only motion.tsx changes, with no new cache, timer, framework or RPC; fresh ownership, continuous 220/160ms and the 450ms handoff are kept. The existing partial collapse on the Samsung goes from about 39 to 26–28ms, expand is unstable, and there is no desktop/FPS gain conclusion. Typecheck, Luna mounted 11/11 and focused 5 files / 37 tests already pass, and the first old-document assertion failure is retained; the local Flowly reload is running, and real UI/FPS was not run. The user asked to stop further testing and merge directly, so the full candidate run and the device matrix were not run again; only this fix and the spec were selected to ship through the PR flow. No tour/tip/What's new needed. Evidence is kept in verification/marquee-width-2026-10-24.

## 2026-10-27 Summary no longer generated: Paseo 0.13's turn_ended carries no timeline

- Symptom: several Agent SMITH cards have no progress. Data: daemon 0.13.0 started on 10-26 at 02:04Z, and since then, among completed turns in all projects, only manual Refresh wrote a summary (Flowly 1/77 on 10-26, 0/18 on 10-27; SMITH 2/27 on 10-27).
- Root cause: Paseo 0.13's `publishAgentStream` always sends `timeline: []` for turn_completed/failed/canceled (the SDK type still declares the field, so the build shows no error). `SummaryService.enqueue` returns straight away on empty text, with no error record. The same empty array also affects `byUser`/`pick` (Agent habits), memory clues, the workpad plan and external-session insights.
- Fix: `server/turn-timeline.ts` reads the last turn of the newest timeline page when the timeline is empty, and the `agent.turn_ended` entry point uses it uniformly; the `byUser` of `agent.turn_ended` was recorded as false in events from 10-26 on and has not been backfilled.



## Codex suggested speed — 2026-10-29

- Codex card-session suggestions now use Fast when no speed is remembered, including provider switches. Explicit Normal and other remembered tiers remain intact; unavailable tiers retain the existing normal fallback.
- No tour/tip or telemetry change: this adjusts the existing Speed control default, adding no capability, switch or counted action.

- Claude defaults to `auto` and Codex to `auto-review` when no permission mode is set; explicit and remembered modes win.

## Suggestions for custom Claude/Codex providers (card "improve model defaults in create task dialog") — 2026-10-30

- Bug: the Auto/Auto-review and Fast suggestions matched only the provider ids `claude` and `codex`, so switching the New task / New session picker to a custom provider (`claude-connected`, `claude-glm-fast`, `codex-connected`) left Permissions on Provider default and Speed on normal.
- Fix: `providerBase` (`shared/agent-defaults.ts`) recognises the base provider from the permission modes; `client/agent-choice.tsx` keeps an unchosen mode/speed as `undefined` and applies the base's suggestion once the modes are listed (submit waits for them). `startingAgent` no longer bakes suggestions in, so a model default on a custom provider gets them too. Explicit picks and remembered session settings still win.
- Live check after the plugin reload found a second bug: Codex's Fast tier is `priority` in the daemon (options default/priority/ultrafast), not `fast`, so the Fast suggestion never matched and every Codex provider, the built-in one too, opened at Normal. `suggestedSpeed` now picks `priority`, else `fast`, from the model's offered tiers.
- Follow-up request: reasoning now defaults to `xhigh` (Extra High) when the model offers it, for every provider (`suggestedThinking`; the picker keeps an unchosen reasoning as `undefined` like mode and speed). Models without it (GLM 5.3) keep their own default; explicit picks and remembered reasoning win. MODEL-DEFAULTS-SPEC "Reasoning default".
- No tour/tip, What's new or telemetry change: same Permissions/Speed/Reasoning rows with different starting values, now also for custom providers. No new strings.
- Checks on HEAD 4c077a0 plus the shared tree's uncommitted work: `npm run typecheck` exit 0; `npm test` 147 files, 1286 passed, 1 skipped. Live after `paseo plugin reload flowly`: `docs/verification/model-defaults-custom-providers.mjs` (headless, form cancelled) switched New task to Codex, Connected / Claude Code, Connected / Claude Code, GLM 5.3 / Codex / Claude and read Auto-review + Fast + xhigh, Auto mode + Extra High, Auto mode + Model default (High), Auto-review + Fast + xhigh, Auto mode + Extra High; no page errors. Evidence in `docs/verification/model-defaults-custom-providers/`.

## 2026-11-01 Android Back key steps back level by level

- User feedback: the Android Back key closes Flowly directly from any position. Per the 2026-11-01 Android Back item of [UI-UX-SPEC §5](UI-UX-SPEC.md#5-phone-and-narrow-layouts): Back and Esc go through the same `runIntent("escape")`, session view → card → page/board, and only at the board root is it handed to Paseo.
- `client/android-back.ts`: subscribes to `BackHandler` on Android only; on each press it first finds, through the React fiber, the react-navigation navigation object of the screen Flowly is on and calls `isFocused()`, and does not take over when an agent/workspace screen pushed by Paseo is on top. When it cannot find it or errors, it returns false and leaves the host behavior alone. The fiber walk is extracted to `client/host-fiber.ts`, shared with `host-horizontal-scroll.ts`. AGENTS.md has gained an exception.
- Not handled (no code added): when the host sidebar covers Flowly, pressing Back closes the card behind it; during the host dialog's presentation animation (about 300 ms), Back is handled by Flowly.
- Onboarding: standard platform behavior, so no tip and no tour change. What's new: not added for a patch release. Telemetry: no field added. No new strings.
- Tests: `client/android-back.test.ts` (nearest screen navigation object, ignoring look-alike objects, a tree that throws).

## 2026-10-31 Swipe left and right to switch lanes on the phone board

- User request: swipe left and right on the phone board to switch lanes, without conflicting with Paseo's swipe that opens the sidebar; following better-paseo, only the leftmost lane triggers the sidebar. Per the 2026-10-31 lane swipe item of [UI-UX-SPEC §5](UI-UX-SPEC.md#5-phone-and-narrow-layouts) (it replaces the earlier rule of "no horizontal paging").
- `client/lane-pager.tsx`: on iOS/Android each lane is a page of a native horizontally paged `ScrollView`, in the same order as the status menu, following the finger, and on release it settles on the nearest lane and updates `mobileStatus`. Only the current lane is mounted; after 400 ms idle, one lane on each side is mounted, and the other pages are empty placeholders. Choosing from the status menu jumps straight there, keeping the original fade-in (`lanePick`). Web is unchanged.
- Sidebar: when leaving the first lane, `client/host-horizontal-scroll.ts` finds Paseo's `HorizontalScrollContext` through the React fiber and registers a horizontal offset of 100, so the host's swipe-right gesture that opens the sidebar fails and the right swipe is handled by the pager. It unregisters when returning to the first lane, when a card covers the board, or on unmount. When it cannot find the registry it does nothing and reports no error. AGENTS.md has gained this exception for relying on Paseo's internals.
- Onboarding: a new one-time tip `lane-swipe` ("Swipe left or right to switch lanes.", 9 catalogs), placed after first-card and before card-display; swiping to another lane also counts as seen. The tour is unchanged. What's new: not added for a patch release. Telemetry: no field added (a pure gesture, not a switch or core action).
- Tests: `client/host-horizontal-scroll.test.ts` (finding the registry, ignoring look-alike objects, a circular tree and a tree that throws), `settledPage` in `lane-rows.test.ts`, and the tip order in `onboarding-logic.test.ts`.

## 2026-11-05 Accessible names and stable testIDs for board cards (for automation targeting)

- Source card: board cards get complete accessible names so that UI automation checks can locate them. Before, a card's accessible name did not include the lane and it had no testID, so Playwright `getByText` only matched the host sidebar's same-named session entry, and clicks on cards often missed when the pager/horizontal scroll landing point was not settled; the contract is set by the 2026-11-04 automation names item of [UI-UX-SPEC §2](UI-UX-SPEC.md#2-board-toolbar-and-secondary-controls) (added in this change).
- `client/card-label.ts` (new, pure module): `taskCardLabel` builds the card's accessible name, starting with "Open task {title}. {lane}." (the lane is the business status, covering "lane + status"), followed by all the existing fact segments; `workpadText` moves in with it. `client/card.tsx` adds `testID="card-{taskId}"` where it renders; `client/board.tsx` passes `lane={nameOf(status)}`, adds `lane-tab-{status}` to the status menu rows and `lane-chooser` to the compact status row; `client/lane-pager.tsx` adds `lane-page-{status}` to its pages; `Row` (`client/ui.tsx`) supports `testID`. `priorityLabels` moves to `shared/model.ts` (alongside statusLabels/taskTypeLabels), and `client/status.tsx` re-exports it in place, so callers are unchanged.
- `docs/promo/shot-lib.mjs`: the Flowly entry and project tab in `open()` now click by exact text within a horizontal band (the sidebar / plugin tab row) and throw when nothing is found, instead of silently staying on the wrong project; the board-ready wait now uses testID/aria-label (language-independent). New `openCard(s, title)` (by title alone: it clicks `getByRole("button", { name: /^Open task {title}\./ })` and waits for `task-drawer-motion` to show that title) and `closeCard(s)` (Esc + waiting for pointer-events to lapse).
- Tests: `client/card-label.test.ts` (title + lane prefix, lane omitted when absent, signal segment order, priority/type segments). Verification script `docs/verification/board-card-open-2026-11-05/run.mjs`: on a real web client, opening and closing a given card's sidebar 5 times in a row by title alone (the real flowly project, since this machine's daemon has never had the Tinylink fixture created); see the report in that directory.
- Onboarding: no UI/entry-point change, so no tour, tip or What's new. Telemetry: no field added. No new user-visible strings (lane names are existing localized data). Performance: only one more string segment and the existing testID property; the card memo comparison includes the lane string.

## 2026-11-10 Delivery mode Ship to main (`ship_main`)
- Fourth delivery mode `ship_main` (ruling by Noah 2026-11-10, `docs/DELIVERY-MODE-SPEC.md` §4 and Ruling): `shared/delivery-mode.ts` adds it to `deliveryModes`, with the label "Ship to main" (translated in the nine catalogs), and `deliveryLine` spells out the checks, the rebase, `flowly_integrate`, `git push origin main` and the stop conditions. Cards, Edit, New task, the host default and the group default (Tooling group) all take the value from `deliveryModes`; the group default goes through the existing `resolveDeliveryMode` chain.
- The ship step after delivery is triggered by Auto review passing: `server/auto-review.ts` gains an optional dependency `deliveryMode`, and when the review passes and the effective mode is `ship_main` it sends the `shipMessage` of `shared/auto-review.ts` (carrying the `[Flowly auto review` marker, not counted as conversation); in every other case no message is sent. `index.server.ts` resolves the mode by card → project → group → host.
- Stop rules (agent execution): a rebase conflict, a failed check after the rebase, a `flowly_integrate` refusal, the main checkout being behind origin, or an open finding leaves the card at In review and reports a blocker; if the push is rejected, it sets integrated and reports. It pushes only main, never forced.
- Tool and skill text: the `flowly_integrate` description, the `done` note and the skill's "Delivery mode" line (`server/skill-source.ts` and `skills/flowly/SKILL.md` kept in sync). The `flowly_integrate` exception in AGENTS.md is updated.
- Tests: `shared/delivery-mode.test.ts` (modes, labels, line content, group resolution and acceptance by taskInput/taskEdit); `server/auto-review.test.ts` (ship_main sends the message on a pass, branch does not). `npm run typecheck`, `npm test` (198 files passed, 1 skipped; 2136 tests passed) and `make check` all pass.
- Onboarding and What's new: not changed (a fourth value, covered together with card 7/7 of Board groups). Telemetry: no field added (delivery mode has no heartbeat field).

## 2026-11-10 New sessions never inherit an earlier session's settings
- Cause: with no project, group or global default, new sessions (New task / New session / Quick task pickers, `flowly_start_card`, so dispatcher starts too) took provider, model, reasoning, mode and speed from the project's latest session, which counted Flowly's own Haiku summarizer sessions; card sessions started on Haiku 5.5 without anyone choosing it. Separately, unset reasoning was suggested as Extra High.
- Change (Noah's decision): `startingAgent(chosen)` takes only the resolved default (project → group → global); `lastSessionSettings` removed (it had no other users). Paseo exposes no default provider/model to plugins, so that step is skipped. No default: the picker starts with Provider and Model empty and the hint "No default model. Set one in Settings → Models." (`pickerSeed`); `flowly_start_card` refuses with `MODEL_UNAVAILABLE` asking for a default in Settings → Models. Reasoning: the default's, else the model's own (`chosenThinking`; `suggestedThinking` removed). Mode: the default's, else `suggestedMode`; Quick task's `plan` default unchanged. Settings labels that promised "latest session" now read "Choose a model when you start a session" and **Automatic** (Auto mode for Claude, Auto-review for Codex). Specs: MODEL-DEFAULTS-SPEC §1, §2, §4, Reasoning default; START-CARD-SPEC §5; AGENT-SPEED-SPEC; QUICK-TASK-SPEC §1; README.
- Tests: `shared/agent-defaults.test.ts` (`startingAgent` null with no default, `pickerSeed` empty, `chosenThinking` never xhigh by itself); `server/start-card.test.ts` (no default → `MODEL_UNAVAILABLE` even with live sessions; a newer Haiku summarizer session in the card's workspace is ignored; no `thinkingOptionId` without a default reasoning). `npm run typecheck` exit 0; `npm test` 206 files passed, 1 skipped; 2269 tests passed, 7 skipped. Not run: live UI check of the empty picker and hint.
- Onboarding: no tour or tip change (a fix to defaults, no new feature). Telemetry: no field. What's new: none (patch).

## 2026-11-11 Board pan never started on web (branch `board-pan-from-cards`)
- [x] User report: dragging the desktop board with the mouse did nothing. Cause (reproduced in headless Chromium against the live board, scrollLeft stayed 0 for drags between lanes and in a lane's empty area): the viewport recorded the press with `onPointerDownCapture`, which RN-web's `View` does not forward to the DOM, so `press` stayed null and `boardPanDecision` always said "none". Setting the press ref through the React fiber made the same drags pan (0→285→570). Fix: bubble-phase `onPointerDown` on the viewport with `boardPress`; a card's slot marks the native event so a card press still never pans. PASS: `npm run typecheck`, `npm test` (211 files, 2376 tests). UNRUN: the fixed bundle in the real app (the installed plugin was not swapped); the in-page simulation of the new handler panned from gaps, empty lane area and lane headers and flagged card presses.
- Onboarding: no tour or tip change (a fix). Telemetry: no field. What's new: none (patch).

## 2026-11-12 Dispatcher replies to agents that wrote to it (branch `dispatcher-agent-replies`)
- Ruling (Noah, 2026-11-12): new preapproved dispatcher tool `flowly_reply_to_agent { agentId, text }`, only to the sender of an `agent_message` the dispatcher's own session received in the last 24 hours; never to itself, another dispatcher or a card's session (`REPLY_NOT_ALLOWED`), never to an agent with a pending request (`SESSION_WAITING_ON_USER`); 5 per agent and 20 in all per hour (`REPLY_LIMIT`); prefixed with `dispatcherMessagePrefix` and steered. `dispatcherWithheldTools` and `dispatcherGuardVersion` unchanged (the guard covers the withheld routes, which did not change; the new tool raises the new-tools warning). Charter updated. DISPATCHER-SPEC §5, §5.4, §6; `docs/verification/dispatcher-tool-allowlist.md`.
- Server: `DispatcherTools.replyToAgent`, `heardFrom` (reads the caller's projected timeline back in pages of 200, at most 10), `reserveReply`. Tests: `server/dispatcher-tools.test.ts`, charter test in `server/dispatcher.test.ts`.
- Not recorded as a card event or in Insights, like `flowly_message_card` (which has no audit path either). Not run against a live dispatcher.
- Onboarding: none (an agent tool, no UI). Telemetry: no field. What's new: none (not a release).

## 2026-11-12 Dispatcher replies reached agents twice (branch `kanban/dispatcher-replies-reach-agents-twice-653331`)
- Cause (verified): Paseo reports a prompted agent's final turn text to a sender notified on finish (default). Checked live: a throwaway Haiku agent's finish report to this session carried its final text verbatim. So `flowly_reply_to_agent` followed by a final text that restates the reply reaches the sender twice. Not reproduced against a live dispatcher (the tool half is covered by its existing tests).
- Fix: charter line and tool description say to answer through the tool or the final text, never both; the tool result carries `note` (`replyOnceNote`) saying not to repeat the reply in the final text. Limits unchanged (24 hours, no card or dispatcher, rate limits, never a card's asks). DISPATCHER-SPEC §5, §5.4.
- Tests: `server/dispatcher-tools.test.ts` (result note), `server/dispatcher.test.ts` (charter); both fail on the previous source and pass now. Onboarding: none. Telemetry: no field. What's new: none.

## 2026-11-12 Board pan no longer expands or collapses lanes
- Cause: a collapsed lane was one `Pressable` whose `onPress` expanded it. RN-web dispatches `onPress` from the DOM click, which the browser still fires after a drag that starts and ends on the same element, so a pan over a collapsed lane (or a drag on the hover-revealed Collapse button) toggled the lane.
- Fix: lanes toggle only through a dedicated button (collapsed lane: always-visible Expand chevron at its top; expanded lane: the existing Collapse button). The collapsed lane is now plain pannable space with the header menu. The board viewport tracks the mouse press (`togglePressStart/Move/End`), and `laneToggleFires` lets a toggle fire only for a press that began on it and never moved `dragThreshold`; keyboard/assistive activation (no tracked press) and touch always fire. `IconButton` gained `expanded`. No new strings. UI-UX-SPEC updated.
- PASS: `npm run typecheck` (no errors); `npm test` (215 files passed, 1 skipped; 2441 tests passed, 7 skipped), including the new `lane toggle press` tests in `client/geometry.test.ts`.
- UNRUN: the real app in a browser (pan over a collapsed lane, drag on a toggle, click on a toggle).

## 2026-11-13 Quick task: Group picker (branch `kanban-add-a-board-group-input-to-the-command-c`)
- Quick task gets a **Group** picker before Project (every entry point, same dialog): the groups with a live project, in position order; hidden with fewer than two and from an idea. It opens on the group of the project the dialog opens with (the shown board's group, or a pinned/fallback project's group); the Project picker lists only the selected group's live projects, replacing "group's projects first, then the others". Switching group picks the last quick-task project when a member, else the first member, and resets the agent. No new setting (Ruling, Noah 2026-11-13). `membersFirst` removed; new `quickGroups`, `quickGroupOf`, `quickGroupProjects`, `quickGroupProject` in `client/board-groups.ts`. One new string (`Group`) in all catalogs. Onboarding: no new tip or tour copy (a small picker in an existing dialog). Telemetry: no heartbeat field. Specs: QUICK-TASK-SPEC §1, §3, §8; BOARD-GROUPS-SPEC §4.
- PASS: `npm run typecheck`; `npm test` (215 files passed, 1 skipped; 2467 tests passed, 7 skipped), including the new Group picker tests in `client/board-groups.test.ts`.
- UNRUN: the real app (picker in the dialog, switching group, desktop and phone).

## 2026-11-14 Dispatcher reads "start X" as create and start a card (branch `kanban/teach-the-dispatcher-that-start-x-means-86c15f`)
- Charter line `startRule`: a user message saying to "start" something means create its card (`ideaId` for a parked idea) and start it with `flowly_start_card`, never a question and never the dispatcher doing the work. Running dispatchers keep their charter and Replace refuses a guarded one with every tool, so every note batch repeats the line before `noteReplyRule`. DISPATCHER-SPEC §6, §7, §10.
- Tests: `server/dispatcher.test.ts` (charter line; note batch carries it), failing before the change. `npm run typecheck` exit 0; `npm test` exit 0 (2472 passed, 7 skipped). Not run against a live dispatcher. Onboarding: none. Telemetry: no field. What's new: none.

## 2026-11-14 Dispatcher card confirm (branch `kanban/confirm-card-creation-in-the-dispatcher-20a7c6`)
- Ruling (Noah, 2026-11-14): the dispatcher's `flowly_create_card` is confirmed by the user on every call, always (not a setting). DISPATCHER-SPEC §5.2, LINEAR-TICKET-SPEC §3–§5.
- `flowly_create_card` moved from `dispatcherToolNames` to `dispatcherConfirmedToolNames` (not preapproved, not saved). A session saved without it runs it only in a mode that asks (`CONFIRMATION_UNAVAILABLE` otherwise, the `flowly_create_project` check); one saved with it preapproved creates as before until replaced. Charter version 2 adds the confirm line, so running dispatchers get the Replace warning.
- Card confirm in the chat (`client/card-confirm.tsx`, logic in `shared/card-confirm.ts`): title, one settings line (project · type · priority · workspace · Linear ask), Cancel / Linear toggle / Create; answers through Paseo's `updatedInput` (`linearTicket: false` or dropped), Cancel denies with `CARD_REFUSED`. `PendingRequests` and `SessionTranscript` take an optional `renderRequest`. The Linear default is the shared `linearTicketAsked` (also used by `linearTicketLines`).
- Onboarding: no tour or tip change; the confirm explains itself where it appears. Telemetry: no heartbeat field.
- Tests (failing first): `shared/card-confirm.test.ts`, `server/dispatcher-tools.test.ts` (new dispatcher not preapproved, mode check, opt-out applied; old dispatcher unchanged), `server/dispatcher.test.ts` (charter line, version 2). `npm run typecheck` exit 0; `npm test` exit 0 on Node 26 (216 files passed, 1 skipped; 2489 passed, 7 skipped). Node 20 lacks `node:sqlite`, so the sqlite and script suites fail there regardless of this change.
- UNRUN: the confirm against a live dispatcher in the app (desktop and phone).
- Review (opus-reviewer, 1e0f167): no blocking findings; request name and `updatedInput` checked against Paseo's Claude provider. Fixed: a session without `savedTools` (open hook, nothing preapproved) now gets the mode check too; `confirmsCards` table test added. Documented: retries ask again and return the card unchanged; another group's project is confirmed then refused; allow rules and "always allow" skip the prompt (as for the other confirmed tools).

## 2026-11-16 Back and Forward history (branch `kanban/back-and-forward-navigation-between-card-2af84d`)
- User request: Slack-style Back / Forward through what the user opened, separate from the unread step-through (which keeps its own Next / Previous). UI-REFRESH-SPEC §7ae (new), §8; SHORTCUTS-SPEC §2.1 (`⌘[` / `⌘]`, Ctrl on other systems; help row); UI-UX-SPEC §5 (Android Back unchanged).
- Client: `client/nav-history.ts` (pure: entries card + session / dispatcher of a group / bare board, `recordLocation`, `navStep`, `canNavStep`, `pruneNavHistory`, `navAlive`, `navOpenAction`; cap 50), `client/use-nav-history.ts` (per-board in-memory state; records every change of the place shown, ignores the places passed on the way to an entry it opens for up to 1.5 s). `client/board.tsx` derives the place (open card, else the dispatcher chat when it shows, else the board, once the overview and settings are read), opens entries through `openTaskById` + the session entry, the group switch + `setDockOpen`, or closing the card / chat, and shows ← / → at the start of the header on desktop and phones. `client/drawer.tsx` reports the session it shows (`onSessionShown`). `client/step-through.ts` unchanged. No Attention view exists any more (`menu` "attention" is unused), so it has no entry.
- Tests (failing first: `client/nav-history.test.ts` could not import the missing module; the new `client/shortcuts.test.ts` case failed on `history-back`): stack, cap, dedupe, session in place, forward truncation, dead entries skipped and pruned both ways, buttons off, walk C → B → A → X, opens only and no read/mark exports, `navAlive` rules; the `⌘[` / `⌘]` keys. PASS: `npm run typecheck` exit 0; `npm test` exit 0 (226 files passed, 1 skipped; 2676 tests passed, 7 skipped), Node 26.
- UNRUN: the real app (header buttons, keys, desktop dock and phone chat page entries, Back through a walk, read counts after revisiting).
- Onboarding: no tour copy or tip (familiar browser-style arrows in the header; the help list names the keys). Telemetry: no heartbeat field. What's new: none (not a release).

## 2026-11-16 Arrow keys for Back and Forward (branch `kanban/arrow-key-shortcuts-for-card-chat-back-a-20c010`)
- User request: `⌥←` / `⌥→` (`Alt+←` / `Alt+→` on other systems) also walk Back / Forward, like `⌘[` / `⌘]`. The first version used `⌘⌥←` / `⌘⌥→`; the user found it too hard to press and chose plain Option+arrows (2026-11-16), so `⌘⌥` is no longer accepted. Ctrl+arrows and Shift chords are not accepted (macOS desktop switch, word move). SHORTCUTS-SPEC §2.1, UI-REFRESH-SPEC §7ae.
- Client: `resolveShortcut` in `client/shortcuts.ts`, a new help row, the header button labels name both combos, the new string is in all nine catalogs. The agent-question `←` / `→` handler (`stepQuestion`) already ignores `Alt`/`Meta`, so the two never both fire (tested).
- Tests: `client/shortcuts.test.ts` (new combos, rejected chords, unique help keys and titles, no clash with `stepQuestion`). PASS: `npm run typecheck` exit 0; `npm test` exit 0 (226 files passed, 1 skipped; 2698 tests passed, 7 skipped), Node 26.
- UNRUN: the keys in the real app on desktop. Onboarding: no tour copy or tip (same action as the existing keys; the help list names them). Telemetry: no heartbeat field. What's new: none.

## 2026-11-16 Drop ⌘[ / ⌘] for Back and Forward (branch `kanban/drop-the-back-forward-shortcuts-that-con-156528`)
- User decision (2026-11-16): `⌘[` / `⌘]` (and `Ctrl+[` / `Ctrl+]`) clash with Paseo's own shortcuts, so Flowly no longer binds them; they go to the host. `⌥←` / `⌥→` (`Alt+←` / `Alt+→` on other systems) are now the only keys for Back / Forward, besides the header buttons. SHORTCUTS-SPEC §2.1, UI-REFRESH-SPEC §7ae.
- Client: `resolveShortcut` drops the bracket branch; the help list drops the `⌘[ ⌘]` row (its string "Back / forward through what you opened" removed from all nine catalogs); the header buttons read "Back (⌥←)" / "Forward (⌥→)" on Apple systems and "Back (Alt+←)" / "Forward (Alt+→)" elsewhere (`historyKeyHint`, `isApplePlatform` in `client/shortcuts.ts`, navigator feature-detected; unknown platforms keep the mac glyphs). No other Flowly shortcut used `⌘[` / `⌘]` (checked `client/`, `shared/`).
- Tests: `client/shortcuts.test.ts` (brackets with ⌘, Ctrl, none or extra modifiers resolve to null; no help row names a bracket; key hints per platform). PASS: `npm run typecheck` exit 0; `npm test` exit 0 (226 files passed, 1 skipped; 2699 tests passed, 7 skipped), Node 26.
- UNRUN: the keys and tooltips in the real app (the installed plugin is updated from main only, after the push). Onboarding: no tour copy or tip (a removed alias; the help list names the keys). Telemetry: no heartbeat field. What's new: none.

## 2026-11-18 Dispatcher asks with AskUserQuestion, not in text (branch `kanban/dispatchers-ask-real-input-requiring-que-e3b081`)
- Ruling (Noah, 2026-11-18): option D of the card: AskUserQuestion joins the dispatcher's built-in tools and charter version 5 says to ask decisions with it (or through a confirmed tool's confirm when the decision is yes/no to that call), never as a question in text, one ask at a time. Rejected: charter-only (open questions keep no buttons), a new `flowly_ask_user` tool (duplicates AskUserQuestion). DISPATCHER-SPEC §5.6, §3, §6.
- `shared/dispatcher.ts`: `dispatcherBuiltinTools` adds `AskUserQuestion`; charter line added; `dispatcherCharterVersion` 4 → 5; `dispatcherGuardVersion` stays 2. The chat, need-you count, held notes and queued peer messages already handled a dispatcher's pending question request; no client or RPC change. Version-0 batch shim unchanged (removal stays planned for 1.10.0; the package is 1.9.0).
- Tests (failing first, then passing): `server/dispatcher.test.ts` (built-in allowlist includes AskUserQuestion; charter line and version 5, guard 2). `npm run typecheck` exit 0; `npm test` exit 0 on Node 26 (226 files passed, 1 skipped; 2712 tests passed, 7 skipped). On Node 20 the `node:sqlite` suites fail to load (environment, not this change).
- Onboarding: no tour or tip change (the dispatcher's asks already show as asks). Telemetry: no heartbeat field.
- Review (opus-reviewer, e674737): no route to a card's ask; AskUserQuestion is never auto-approved in bypassPermissions or auto (Claude Code `requiresUserInteraction`, Paseo's bypass approval skips question tools; read in the installed code, not run). Fixed: the charter no longer asks from a board-update or peer-message turn (a waiting question would hold notes and peer messages), falls back to one line of text when the ask is refused (dontAsk), defers to `startRule`, and names AskUserQuestion for `BASE_CHOICE_NEEDED`; spec wording on modes corrected; stray `package-lock.json` churn reverted. The version test of charter 4 now reads `>= 4` (the new test pins 5).
